# Claim ledger: algo-think levels 1 to 3, round 3 blind audit (13 Nov 2026)

Scope: l1 to l14, review-1 to review-3, homework-1 to homework-3 at commit 0a98e065. Stage 1, full depth: one row for every prose, question, accept, option reason, hint, feedback line, explain, summary line, model answer and rubric line.

How the rows were judged. Every item was read in full and each number, count, bound and code claim in it recomputed; the basis column gives the derivation, source or run that covers the row's item. "script" is `docs/reports/algo-think-L1-3-r3-concepts.py` (CPython 3.13.18; 311 checks). "Pyodide run" is Pyodide 0.27.7 (Python 3.12.7, wasm32) under node 22, recursion limit 3000, with the page's 5 second limit applied by hand. All 41 code widgets: solution passes and starter fails in both. 97 submissions were written for the feedback.byKey lines that name or imply a mistake; the first failing test of each was compared with the key the line sits under. Rows that are not `correct` carry their own basis and a finding number.

Rows: 2621. Verdicts: correct 2601, wrong 0, imprecise 17, misleading 2, unverified 1.
